const hash = (value: string | Buffer) =>
  createHash("sha256").update(value).digest("hex");
it("runs the real CLI, committed policy and private context receipt factories through an isolated V2 delivery", async () => {
  root = realpathSync(await createScratchDir("tests", "delivery-entrypoint"));
  repo = join(root, "repo");
  await cp(
    resolve(
      dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)),
      "../../../fixtures/guarded-local",
    ),
    repo,
    { recursive: true },
  );
  const home = join(root, "home");
  await mkdir(home);
  vi.stubEnv("OSSTACK_HOME", home);
  vi.stubEnv("OSSTACK_CONFIG", join(home, "absent.yaml"));
  const git = (...args: string[]) =>
    execFileSync("git", ["-C", repo!, ...args], {
      encoding: "utf8",
      stdio: "pipe",
    }).trim();
  git("init", "-b", "main");
  await mkdir(join(repo, ".osstack"), { recursive: true });
  await writeFile(
    join(repo, ".osstack/repo.yaml"),
    JSON.stringify({
      schema_version: 2,
      product: "entrypoint",
      role: "core_product",
      default_branch: "main",
      verify_command: "pnpm test",
      autonomy: {
        max_auto_risk: "R2",
        require_independent_evaluator: true,
        max_builder_iterations: 1,
        max_review_rounds: 1,
        max_wall_time_minutes: 2,
        max_no_progress_rounds: 1,
      },
      git: {
        remote: "origin",
        branch_prefix: "codex",
        merge_strategy: "squash",
        auto_merge: false,
        protected_branches: ["main"],
      },
      verification: {
        quick: ["pnpm test"],
        standard: ["pnpm test"],
        release: ["pnpm test"],
      },
    }),
  );
  await writeFile(
    join(repo, ".osstack/policy.yaml"),
    JSON.stringify({
      schema_version: 1,
      mode: "execute",
      auto_apply: true,
      allowed_auto_changes: ["docs", "tests"],
      requires_approval: ["source"],
      forbidden: ["secrets"],
      budgets: { max_iterations: 5, max_runtime_minutes: 45, max_cost_usd: 20 },
    }),
  );
  const pack = JSON.stringify({
    id: "local/cache",
    version: "1",
    name: "Cache",
    license: "MIT",
    source_kind: "internal",
    quality_score: 90,
    scope: "project",
    chunks: [
      { chunk_id: "cache", title: "Cache", body: "Cache isolation reference" },
    ],
  });
  await writeFile(join(repo, "pack.json"), pack);
  const context = join(repo, "context.json");
  await writeFile(
    context,
    JSON.stringify({
      schema_version: 1,
      mode: "local",
      repo_path: repo,
      default_tenant: true,
      packs: [
        {
          path: "pack.json",
          pack_id: "local/cache",
          version: "1",
          sha256: hash(pack),
          scope: "project",
          verified_at_ms: Date.now() - 1000,
          expires_at_ms: Date.now() + 120000,
        },
      ],
    }),
  );
  git("add", ".");
  git(
    "-c",
    "user.name=Test",
    "-c",
    "user.email=test@example.invalid",
    "commit",
    "-m",
    "fixture",
  );
  const base = git("rev-parse", "HEAD");
  const source = await readFile(join(repo, "src/greeting.ts"), "utf8");
  const policy = await createDeliverCommandDeps().deliveryContractPolicy!(repo);
  const contract = join(root, "contract.json");
  await writeFile(
    contract,
    JSON.stringify({
      schema_version: 2,
      contract_id: "entrypoint",
      task_id: "entrypoint-task",
      canonical_repo: repo,
      mode: "implement",
      base_head: base,
      goal: "Improve cache isolation",
      required_checks: [
        { id: "tests", registry_ref: deliveryCheckReference("pnpm test") },
      ],
      review_policy_ref: "profile:mac-mini",
      requested_completion: "local_verified",
      allowed_paths: ["src"],
      requested_actions: ["build", "verify"],
      budget_ref: policy.budgetRef,
      policy_digest: policy.policyDigest,
      publication_scope: { mode: "none" },
      merge_authority: { mode: "none" },
    }),
  );
  const prompts = new Map<string, string>();
  const identity = (id: string) => ({
    adapter_id: id,
    adapter_version: "1.0.0",
    auth_mode: "test",
    provider_id: id === "claude-code" ? "anthropic" : "kimi",
    model_id: id === "claude-code" ? "claude-current" : "kimi-code/k3",
    evidence_source: "reported" as const,
  });
  ports.adapters = ["claude-code", "kimi-code"].map((id) => ({
    id,
    display_name: id,
    cost_tier: "mid" as const,
    capabilities: () => [
      CapabilitySchema.parse({
        id: id + ".test",
        agent_id: id,
        kind: id === "claude-code" ? "code.write" : "code.review",
        description: id,
        cost_tier: "mid",
      }),
    ],
    isAvailable: async () => ({ available: true, last_checked_at: 1 }),
    runtimeIdentity: async () => identity(id),
    async *invoke(task: AgentTask): AsyncIterable<HermesEvent> {
      prompts.set(task.run_id, task.prompt);
      if (id === "claude-code") {
        candidate = String(task.context?.["repo_path"]);
        await writeFile(
          join(candidate, "src/greeting.ts"),
          "\nexport const isolated = true;\n",
          { flag: "a" },
        );
      }
      yield {
        event_id: task.run_id + ":1",
        seq: 1,
        run_id: task.run_id,
        session_id: task.session_id,
        created_at: Date.now() / 1000,
        terminal: true,
        type: "done",
        payload: {
          status: "completed",
          last_seq: 0,
          result_summary: "complete",
          ...(id === "kimi-code"
            ? {
                review: {
                  schema_version: 1,
                  verdict: "approved",
                  risk: "read_only",
                  markdown: "Approved",
                  judgments: [],
                  findings: [],
                  recommended_actions: [],
                  evidence_refs: [],
                  runtime_identity: identity(id),
                },
              }
            : {}),
        },
      };
    },
  }));
  const logs: string[] = [];
  const errors: string[] = [];
  vi.spyOn(console, "log").mockImplementation((...args) => {
    logs.push(args.map(String).join(" "));
  });
  vi.spyOn(console, "error").mockImplementation((...args) => {
    errors.push(args.map(String).join(" "));
  });
  const code = await main([
    "deliver",
    "--workspace",
    repo,
    "--profile",
    "mac-mini",
    "--autonomy",
    "guarded-local",
    "--contract",
    contract,
    "--execution-context",
    context,
    "--json",
  ]);
  expect(code, logs.join("\n") + errors.join("\n")).toBe(0);
  const result = JSON.parse(logs.join("\n"));
  expect(result.delivery_contract.achieved_completion).toBe("local_verified");
  expect(git("rev-parse", "HEAD")).toBe(base);
  expect(await readFile(join(repo, "src/greeting.ts"), "utf8")).toBe(source);
  expect(git("status", "--porcelain")).toBe("");
  expect(result.json_path.startsWith(join(home, "delivery-contracts"))).toBe(true);
  expect(result.task_path.startsWith(join(home, "delivery-contracts"))).toBe(true);
  const receipt = JSON.parse(
    await readFile(result.delivery_contract.verification_receipt.path, "utf8"),
  );
  expect(receipt.context_receipt_refs.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(4);
  for (const ref of receipt.context_receipt_refs) {
    const bytes = await readFile(ref.path);
    expect(hash(bytes)).toBe(ref.digest);
    expect(ref.path.startsWith(join(home, "execution-context-receipts"))).toBe(
      true,
    );
    const recorded = JSON.parse(bytes.toString());
    expect(recorded.prompt_sha256).toBe(hash(prompts.get(recorded.run_id)!));
    expect(recorded.contract_digest).toBe(result.delivery_contract.digest);
  }
  expect(
    [...prompts.values()].some((prompt) =>
      prompt.includes("Cache isolation reference"),
    ),
  ).toBe(true);
}, 60000);


FILE packages/cli/src/delivery-policy-host.test.ts
import { execFileSync } from "node:child_process";
import { mkdirSync, realpathSync, rmSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { createScratchDirSync } from "@osstack/core";
import { afterEach, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { createDeliverCommandDeps } from "./bin.js";
const roots: string[] = [];
afterEach(() => {
  vi.unstubAllEnvs();
  for (const root of roots.splice(0))
    rmSync(root, { recursive: true, force: true });
});
it("binds V2 checks and budget to committed host policy without enabling auto-merge", async () => {
  const root = realpathSync(
    createScratchDirSync("tests", "delivery-host-policy"),
  );
  roots.push(root);
  const home = join(root, "home");
  mkdirSync(home);
  vi.stubEnv("OSSTACK_HOME", home);
  mkdirSync(join(root, ".osstack"));
  const git = (...args: string[]) =>
    execFileSync("git", ["-C", root, ...args], { stdio: "pipe" });
  git("init", "-b", "main");
  git("config", "user.name", "Test");
  git("config", "user.email", "test@example.invalid");
  const config = {
    schema_version: 2,
    product: "test-policy",
    role: "core_product",
    default_branch: "main",
    verify_command: "node --version",
    autonomy: {
      max_auto_risk: "R2",
      require_independent_evaluator: true,
      max_builder_iterations: 3,
      max_review_rounds: 2,
      max_wall_time_minutes: 15,
      max_no_progress_rounds: 1,
    },
    git: {
      remote: "origin",
      branch_prefix: "codex",
      merge_strategy: "squash",
      auto_merge: false,
      protected_branches: ["main"],
    },
    verification: {
      quick: ["node --version"],
      standard: ["node --version"],
      release: ["node --version"],
    },
  };
  writeFileSync(join(root, ".osstack/repo.yaml"), JSON.stringify(config));
  writeFileSync(
    join(root, ".osstack/policy.yaml"),
    JSON.stringify({
      schema_version: 1,
      mode: "execute",
      auto_apply: true,
      allowed_auto_changes: ["docs", "tests"],
      requires_approval: ["source"],
      forbidden: ["secrets"],
      budgets: { max_iterations: 5, max_runtime_minutes: 45, max_cost_usd: 20 },
    }),
  );
  git("add", ".osstack");
  git("commit", "-m", "policy");
  const deps = createDeliverCommandDeps();
  const base = git("rev-parse", "HEAD").toString().trim();
  const first = await deps.deliveryContractPolicy!(root);
  expect(first).toMatchObject({
    verifyCommands: ["node --version"],
    budgetRef: `policy:${first.policyDigest}`,
    budget: {
      max_builder_iterations: 3,
      max_review_rounds: 2,
      max_wall_time_ms: 900000,
      max_no_progress_rounds: 1,
    },
  });
  writeFileSync(
    join(root, ".osstack/repo.yaml"),
    JSON.stringify({
      ...config,
      verification: {
        ...config.verification,
        standard: ["uncommitted-untrusted-command"],
      },
    }),
  );
  expect(await deps.deliveryContractPolicy!(root)).toEqual(first);
  git("add", ".osstack/repo.yaml");
  git("commit", "-m", "updated policy");
  expect(await deps.deliveryContractPolicy!(root, base)).toEqual(first);
  await expect(deps.deliveryContractPolicy!(root, "HEAD:untrusted")).rejects.toThrow("invalid_policy_revision");
  expect((await deps.deliveryContractPolicy!(root)).policyDigest).not.toBe(
    first.policyDigest,
  );
});

import { execFileSync } from "node:child_process";
import { mkdtempSync, realpathSync } from "node:fs";
import {
  cp,
  readFile,
  rm,
  stat,
  writeFile,
} from "node:fs/promises";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
import { dirname, join, resolve } from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import {
  KimiCodeAdapter,
  type AgentAdapter,
  type AgentRuntimeIdentity,
  type AgentTask,
} from "@osstack/agents";
import {
  CapabilitySchema,
  deliveryCheckReference,
  type Capability,
  type CapabilityKind,
} from "@osstack/core";
import type { HermesEvent } from "@osstack/hermes-adapter";
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import {
  createDeliverCommandDeps,
  main,
} from "./bin.js";
import { persistDeliveryHostRecord, deliveryBytesDigest } from "./delivery-contract-host.js";
import {
  DeliverAutonomyMode,
  handleDeliverCommand,
} from "./deliver-command.js";

let tmp: string;
let logs: string[];
let errors: string[];

beforeEach(() => {
  tmp = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "osstack-deliver-cli-"));
  vi.stubEnv("OSSTACK_HOME", join(tmp, "home"));
  logs = [];
  errors = [];
  vi.spyOn(console, "log").mockImplementation((...args) => {
    logs.push(args.map(String).join(" "));
  });
  vi.spyOn(console, "error").mockImplementation((...args) => {
    errors.push(args.map(String).join(" "));
  });
});

afterEach(async () => {
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
  vi.unstubAllEnvs();
  await rm(tmp, { recursive: true, force: true });
});

describe("deliver CLI", () => {
  it("accepts all guarded autonomy modes", () => {
    expect(DeliverAutonomyMode.options).toEqual([
      "legacy",
      "guarded-local",
      "guarded-git",
      "guarded-deploy",
    ]);
    expect(DeliverAutonomyMode.safeParse("guarded-git").success).toBe(
      true,
    );
  });

  it("preserves bare deliver legacy output without probing adapters", async () => {
    const kimiProbe = vi.spyOn(KimiCodeAdapter.prototype, "isAvailable");

    const exitCode = await main([
      "deliver",
      "Freeze legacy delivery",
      "--workspace",
      tmp,
      "--execute",
      "node -e \"process.exit(0)\"",
      "--json",
    ]);

    expect(exitCode).toBe(0);
    const summary = JSON.parse(logs.join("\n")) as Record<string, unknown>;
    expect(summary).toMatchObject({
      prompt: "Freeze legacy delivery",
      stages: ["intake", "plan", "execute-loop", "verify-review", "proof"],
      verify_commands: [],
      completed_tasks: [expect.objectContaining({ outcome: "success" })],
    });
    expect(summary).not.toHaveProperty("profile");
    expect(summary).not.toHaveProperty("delivery_contract");
    expect(kimiProbe).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(errors).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("runs a product-entry delivery flow and writes report, scorecard, and proof", async () => {
    const exitCode = await main([
      "deliver",
      "Add billing dashboard",
      "--workspace",
      tmp,
      "--execute",
      "node -e \"require('fs').writeFileSync('billing.txt', 'ok')\"",
      "--verify",
      "node -e \"process.exit(require('fs').existsSync('billing.txt') ? 0 : 1)\"",
      "--json",
    ]);

    expect(exitCode).toBe(0);
    const summary = JSON.parse(logs.join("\n")) as {
      prompt: string;
      task_path: string;
      verify_commands: string[];
      stages: string[];
      artifacts: {
        report?: string;
        scorecard_markdown?: string;
        scorecard_html?: string;
        proof_dir?: string;
        proof_zip?: string;
        proof_manifest?: string;
      };
      completed_tasks: Array<{ outcome: string }>;
    };
    expect(summary).toMatchObject({
      prompt: "Add billing dashboard",
      verify_commands: ["node -e \"process.exit(require('fs').existsSync('billing.txt') ? 0 : 1)\""],
      stages: ["intake", "plan", "execute-loop", "verify-review", "proof"],
      completed_tasks: [expect.objectContaining({ outcome: "success" })],
    });
    expect(summary.task_path).toMatch(/tasks\/task_[a-f0-9]{8}\.json$/);
    await expect(readFile(summary.task_path, "utf-8")).resolves.toContain("Add billing dashboard");
    await expect(readFile(summary.artifacts.report!, "utf-8")).resolves.toContain("# Osstack Loop Report");
    await expect(readFile(summary.artifacts.scorecard_html!, "utf-8")).resolves.toContain("<!doctype html>");
    await expect(readFile(summary.artifacts.proof_manifest!, "utf-8")).resolves.toContain("\"kind\": \"osstack.proof_pack\"");
    await expect(stat(summary.artifacts.proof_zip!)).resolves.toMatchObject({ size: expect.any(Number) });
  });

  it("records governed delivery findings without blocking by default", async () => {
    await writeFile(join(tmp, "unsafe.ts"), "const api_key = \"sk-test-secret-token\";\n");

    const exitCode = await main([
      "deliver",
      "Govern existing workspace",
      "--workspace",
      tmp,
      "--execute",
      "node -e \"process.exit(0)\"",
      "--govern",
      "--json",
    ]);

    expect(exitCode).toBe(0);
    const summary = JSON.parse(logs.join("\n")) as {
      governance: { mode: string; status: string; blocking: boolean; findings: Array<{ rule_id: string }> };
      artifacts: { report: string; proof_dir: string };
    };
    expect(summary.governance).toMatchObject({
      mode: "warn",
      status: "fail",
      blocking: false,
      findings: [expect.objectContaining({ rule_id: "secret.api_key" })],
    });
    await expect(readFile(summary.artifacts.report, "utf-8")).resolves.toContain("governance-verifier");
    await expect(readFile(join(summary.artifacts.proof_dir, "governance-results.json"), "utf-8")).resolves.toContain("secret.api_key");
  });

  it("records legacy findings as baseline evidence and gates only new findings", async () => {
    execFileSync("git", ["init", "-b", "main"], { cwd: tmp });
    await writeFile(
      join(tmp, "legacy-unsafe.ts"),
      "const api_key = \"sk-test-secret-token\";\n",
    );
    commitFixture(tmp);

    const exitCode = await main([
      "deliver",
      "Govern only changes from the accepted baseline",
      "--workspace",
      tmp,
      "--execute",
      "node -e \"process.exit(0)\"",
      "--govern",
      "strict",
      "--govern-scope",
      "baseline",
      "--json",
    ]);

    expect(exitCode).toBe(0);
    const summary = JSON.parse(logs.join("\n")) as {
      governance: {
        scope: string;
        blocking: boolean;
        findings: Array<{ rule_id: string }>;
        baseline_evidence: {
          blocking: boolean;
          findings: Array<{ rule_id: string }>;
        };
      };
    };
    expect(summary.governance).toMatchObject({
      scope: "baseline",
      blocking: false,
      findings: [],
      baseline_evidence: {
        blocking: false,
        findings: [
          expect.objectContaining({ rule_id: "secret.api_key" }),
        ],
      },
    });
  });

  it("creates a shadow checkpoint before a non-worktree delivery run", async () => {
    process.env["OSSTACK_HOME"] = join(tmp, "home");
    await writeFile(join(tmp, "app.txt"), "before\n");

    const exitCode = await main([
      "deliver",
      "Checkpoint delivery",
      "--workspace",
      tmp,
      "--execute",
      "node -e \"require('fs').writeFileSync('app.txt', 'after\\n')\"",
      "--verify",
      "node -e \"process.exit(require('fs').readFileSync('app.txt','utf8') === 'after\\n' ? 0 : 1)\"",
      "--checkpoint",
      "--json",
    ]);

    expect(exitCode).toBe(0);
    const summary = JSON.parse(logs.join("\n")) as {
      checkpoint: { checkpoint_id: string; checkpoint_dir: string };
    };
    expect(summary.checkpoint.checkpoint_id).toMatch(/^chk_/);
    await expect(readFile(join(tmp, "app.txt"), "utf-8")).resolves.toBe("after\n");
    await expect(readFile(join(summary.checkpoint.checkpoint_dir, "files", "app.txt"), "utf-8"))
      .resolves.toBe("before\n");
  });

  it("fails strict governed delivery runs on error severity findings", async () => {
    await writeFile(join(tmp, "unsafe.ts"), "const api_key = \"sk-test-secret-token\";\n");

    const exitCode = await main([
      "deliver",
      "Strict govern existing workspace",
      "--workspace",
      tmp,
      "--execute",
      "node -e \"process.exit(0)\"",
      "--govern",
      "strict",
      "--json",
    ]);

    expect(exitCode).toBe(2);
    const summary = JSON.parse(logs.join("\n")) as {
      governance: { mode: string; status: string; blocking: boolean };
      artifacts: { proof_dir: string };
    };
    expect(summary.governance).toMatchObject({ mode: "strict", status: "fail", blocking: true });
    const governance = JSON.parse(await readFile(join(summary.artifacts.proof_dir, "governance-results.json"), "utf-8")) as {
      blocking: boolean;
      findings: Array<{ rule_id: string; severity: string }>;
    };
    expect(governance).toMatchObject({
      blocking: true,
      findings: [expect.objectContaining({ rule_id: "secret.api_key", severity: "error" })],
    });
  });

  it("suggests a verify command from package.json and records it in task context", async () => {
    await writeFile(join(tmp, "package.json"), JSON.stringify({
      scripts: { test: "vitest run" },
    }, null, 2));

    const exitCode = await main([
      "deliver",
      "Wire user settings",
      "--workspace",
      tmp,
      "--execute",
      "node -e \"process.exit(0)\"",
      "--json",
    ]);

    expect(exitCode).toBe(0);
    const summary = JSON.parse(logs.join("\n")) as {
      task_path: string;
      verify_commands: string[];
      suggested_verify: string;
    };
    expect(summary.suggested_verify).toBe("npm test");
    expect(summary.verify_commands).toEqual([]);
    const task = JSON.parse(await readFile(summary.task_path, "utf-8")) as {
      context: { deliver_pipeline: { suggested_verify: string } };
    };
    expect(task.context.deliver_pipeline.suggested_verify).toBe("npm test");
  });

  it("runs guarded-local with separated profile roles and local-only evidence", async () => {
    const workspace = await initGuardedFixture();
    const builderInvoke = vi.fn(async function* (
      task: AgentTask,
    ): AsyncIterable<HermesEvent> {
      const repoPath = task.context?.["repo_path"];
      if (typeof repoPath !== "string") {
        throw new Error("builder task did not include repo_path");
      }
      await writeFile(
        join(repoPath, "src/greeting.ts"),
        "\nexport const guarded = true;\n",
        { flag: "a" },
      );
      yield doneEvent(task, "completed", "builder completed");
    });
    const adapters = guardedAdapters(builderInvoke);
    const remotePorts = {
      push: vi.fn(async () => undefined),
      createPullRequest: vi.fn(async () => undefined),
      merge: vi.fn(async () => undefined),
      deploy: vi.fn(async () => undefined),
    };

    const exitCode = await handleDeliverCommand([
      "Add guarded fixture marker",
      "--workspace",
      workspace,
      "--profile",
      "mac-mini",
      "--autonomy",
      "guarded-local",
      "--json",
    ], {
      ...createDeliverCommandDeps(),
      adapters: () => adapters,
      remotePorts,
    });

    expect(
      exitCode,
      `logs=${logs.join("\n")}\nerrors=${errors.join("\n")}`,
    ).toBe(0);
    const summary = JSON.parse(logs.join("\n")) as {
      autonomy: string;
      profile: {
        builder_agent_id: string;
        evaluator_agent_id: string;
        independent: boolean;
      };
      verify_commands: string[];
      events: Array<{ type: string }>;
      artifacts: { proof_dir?: string; proof_manifest?: string };
      governance: { mode: string; blocking: boolean };
    };
    expect(summary).toMatchObject({
      autonomy: "guarded-local",
      profile: {
        builder_agent_id: "claude-code",
        evaluator_agent_id: "kimi-code",
        independent: true,
      },
      verify_commands: ["pnpm test"],
      governance: { mode: "strict", blocking: false },
    });
    expect(summary.events).toContainEqual(
      expect.objectContaining({ type: "loop.worktree.created" }),
    );
    expect(summary.artifacts.proof_dir).toEqual(expect.any(String));
    await expect(stat(summary.artifacts.proof_manifest!)).resolves
      .toMatchObject({ size: expect.any(Number) });
    await expect(
      readFile(
        join(summary.artifacts.proof_dir!, "policy-decision.json"),
        "utf-8",
      ),
    ).resolves.toContain("\"profile_name\": \"mac-mini\"");
    const runtimeEvidence = JSON.parse(
      await readFile(
        join(summary.artifacts.proof_dir!, "agent-runtime.json"),
        "utf-8",
      ),
    ) as {
      agents: Array<{
        adapter_id?: string;
        provider_id?: string;
        model_id?: string;
      }>;
    };
    expect(runtimeEvidence.agents).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining([
      expect.objectContaining({
        adapter_id: "claude-code",
        provider_id: "anthropic",
        model_id: "claude-current",
      }),
      expect.objectContaining({
        adapter_id: "kimi-code",
        provider_id: "kimi",
        model_id: "kimi-code/k3",
      }),
    ]));
    expect(remotePorts.push).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(remotePorts.createPullRequest).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(remotePorts.merge).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(remotePorts.deploy).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("dispatches guarded-git through the durable runtime and surfaces approval", async () => {
    const workspace = await initGuardedFixture();
    const builderInvoke = vi.fn(async function* (
      task: AgentTask,
    ): AsyncIterable<HermesEvent> {
      yield doneEvent(task, "completed", "unexpected");
    });
    const startGuardedGit = vi.fn(async (_input: unknown) => ({
      status: "needs_approval" as const,
      run: {
        autonomy_run_id: "run-auth",
        state: "awaiting_approval" as const,
        revision: 3,
        context: {},
      },
      approval_request: {
        approval_id: "approval:run-auth:preflight",
        autonomy_run_id: "run-auth",
        phase: "preflight" as const,
        contract_sha256: "a".repeat(64),
        diff_sha256: "0".repeat(64),
        action_scope: ["build", "verify"],
        risk: "R3" as const,
        nonce: "nonce-auth",
        requested_at: "2026-07-28T18:00:00.000Z",
        expires_at: "2026-07-28T19:00:00.000Z",
        request_sha256: "b".repeat(64),
      },
    }));

    const exitCode = await handleDeliverCommand([
      "Change src/auth/session.ts",
      "--workspace",
      workspace,
      "--profile",
      "mac-mini",
      "--autonomy",
      "guarded-git",
      "--expected-path",
      "src/auth/session.ts",
      "--json",
    ], {
      ...createDeliverCommandDeps(),
      adapters: () => guardedAdapters(builderInvoke),
      startGuardedGit,
    });

    expect(exitCode).toBe(2);
    expect(startGuardedGit).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      expect.objectContaining({
        prompt: "Change src/auth/session.ts",
        workspacePath: realpathSync(workspace),
        expectedPaths: ["src/auth/session.ts"],
        requestedActions: [
          "build",
          "commit",
          "merge",
          "push",
          "verify",
        ],
        verifyCommands: ["pnpm test"],
        profileRouting: expect.objectContaining({
          agentId: "claude-code",
          verifierAgentIds: ["kimi-code"],
        }),
      }),
    );
    expect(builderInvoke).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(JSON.parse(logs.join("\n"))).toMatchObject({
      autonomy: "guarded-git",
      autonomy_run_id: "run-auth",
      state: "awaiting_approval",
      status: "needs_approval",
      approval_request: {
        phase: "preflight",
      },
    });
  });

  it("requires project policy and dispatches guarded-deploy through the durable runtime", async () => {
    const workspace = await initGuardedFixture();
    const startGuardedGit = vi.fn(async (_input: unknown) => ({
      status: "completed" as const,
      run: {
        autonomy_run_id: "run-deploy",
        state: "completed" as const,
        revision: 15,
        context: {
          deploy_outcome: "staging_healthy" as const,
        },
      },
    }));
    const builderInvoke = vi.fn(async function* (
      task: AgentTask,
    ): AsyncIterable<HermesEvent> {
      yield doneEvent(task, "completed", "unexpected");
    });

    const missingPolicy = await handleDeliverCommand([
      "Deploy allowlisted source",
      "--workspace",
      workspace,
      "--profile",
      "mac-mini",
      "--autonomy",
      "guarded-deploy",
      "--json",
    ], {
      ...createDeliverCommandDeps(),
      adapters: () => guardedAdapters(builderInvoke),
      startGuardedGit,
    });
    expect(missingPolicy).toBe(2);
    expect(JSON.parse(logs.join("\n"))).toMatchObject({
      error: { code: "deploy_policy_required" },
    });
    logs = [];

    const exitCode = await handleDeliverCommand([
      "Deploy allowlisted source",
      "--workspace",
      workspace,
      "--profile",
      "mac-mini",
      "--autonomy",
      "guarded-deploy",
      "--deploy-policy",
      "project",
      "--json",
    ], {
      ...createDeliverCommandDeps(),
      adapters: () => guardedAdapters(builderInvoke),
      startGuardedGit,
    });

    expect(exitCode).toBe(0);
    expect(startGuardedGit).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      expect.objectContaining({
        deployPolicy: "project",
        requestedActions: [
          "build",
          "commit",
          "deploy:production",
          "deploy:staging",
          "merge",
          "push",
          "verify",
        ],
      }),
    );
    expect(JSON.parse(logs.join("\n"))).toMatchObject({
      autonomy: "guarded-deploy",
      status: "completed",
      deploy_outcome: "staging_healthy",
    });
  });

  it("blocks guarded-local before Builder invoke when verification cannot be detected", async () => {
    const workspace = join(tmp, "missing-verify");
    execFileSync("git", ["init", "-b", "main", workspace]);
    await writeFile(join(workspace, "README.md"), "# fixture\n");
    commitFixture(workspace);
    const builderInvoke = vi.fn(async function* (
      task: AgentTask,
    ): AsyncIterable<HermesEvent> {
      yield doneEvent(task, "completed", "unexpected");
    });

    const exitCode = await handleDeliverCommand([
      "Attempt unverified change",
      "--workspace",
      workspace,
      "--profile",
      "mac-mini",
      "--autonomy",
      "guarded-local",
      "--json",
    ], {
      ...createDeliverCommandDeps(),
      adapters: () => guardedAdapters(builderInvoke),
    });

    expect(exitCode).toBe(2);
    expect(JSON.parse(logs.join("\n"))).toMatchObject({
      error: {
        code: "verify_profile_missing",
      },
    });
    expect(builderInvoke).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("fails closed at delivery execution when no independent Evaluator is available", async () => {
    const workspace = await initGuardedFixture();
    const builderInvoke = vi.fn(async function* (
      task: AgentTask,
    ): AsyncIterable<HermesEvent> {
      yield doneEvent(task, "completed", "unexpected");
    });

    const exitCode = await handleDeliverCommand([
      "Attempt guarded delivery without Kimi",
      "--workspace",
      workspace,
      "--verify",
      "node --test",
      "--profile",
      "mac-mini",
      "--autonomy",
      "guarded-local",
      "--worktree",
      "--json",
    ], {
      ...createDeliverCommandDeps(),
      adapters: () => guardedAdapters(builderInvoke).filter(
        (adapter) => adapter.id !== "kimi-code",
      ),
    });

    expect(exitCode).toBe(2);
    expect(JSON.parse(logs.join("\n"))).toMatchObject({
      error: {
        code: "profile_routing_failed",
        message: expect.stringContaining(
          "no independent Evaluator is available",
        ),
      },
    });
    expect(builderInvoke).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("rejects an invalid guarded-local command timeout before Builder invoke", async () => {
    const workspace = await initGuardedFixture();
    const builderInvoke = vi.fn(async function* (
      task: AgentTask,
    ): AsyncIterable<HermesEvent> {
      yield doneEvent(task, "completed", "unexpected");
    });

    const exitCode = await handleDeliverCommand([
      "Attempt guarded delivery with an invalid timeout",
      "--workspace",
      workspace,
      "--verify",
      "node --test",
      "--profile",
      "mac-mini",
      "--autonomy",
      "guarded-local",
      "--command-timeout-ms",
      "0",
      "--json",
    ], {
      ...createDeliverCommandDeps(),
      adapters: () => guardedAdapters(builderInvoke),
    });

    expect(exitCode).toBe(1);
    expect(errors.join("\n")).toContain(
      "--command-timeout-ms must be a positive integer",
    );
    expect(builderInvoke).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("keeps explicit legacy mode on the frozen no-profile path", async () => {
    const kimiProbe = vi.spyOn(
      KimiCodeAdapter.prototype,
      "isAvailable",
    );

    const exitCode = await main([
      "deliver",
      "Explicit legacy delivery",
      "--workspace",
      tmp,
      "--execute",
      "node -e \"process.exit(0)\"",
      "--autonomy",
      "legacy",
      "--json",
    ]);

    expect(exitCode).toBe(0);
    const summary = JSON.parse(logs.join("\n")) as Record<string, unknown>;
    expect(summary).not.toHaveProperty("profile");
    expect(summary).not.toHaveProperty("autonomy");
    expect(kimiProbe).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});

const guardedFixture = resolve(
  dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)),
  "../../../fixtures/guarded-local",
);

async function initGuardedFixture(): Promise<string> {
  const workspace = join(tmp, "guarded-fixture");
  await cp(guardedFixture, workspace, { recursive: true });
  execFileSync("git", ["init", "-b", "main"], { cwd: workspace });
  commitFixture(workspace);
  return workspace;
}

function commitFixture(workspace: string): void {
  execFileSync("git", ["add", "."], { cwd: workspace });
  execFileSync("git", [
    "-c",
    "user.name=osstack test",
    "-c",
    "user.email=osstack-test@example.invalid",
    "commit",
    "-m",
    "test: seed guarded fixture",
  ], { cwd: workspace });
}

function guardedAdapters(
  builderInvoke: AgentAdapter["invoke"],
): AgentAdapter[] {
  return [
    fakeAdapter({
      id: "claude-code",
      kinds: ["code.write"],
      identity: runtimeIdentity(
        "claude-code",
        "anthropic",
        "claude-current",
      ),
      invoke: builderInvoke,
    }),
    fakeAdapter({
      id: "kimi-code",
      kinds: ["code.review"],
      identity: runtimeIdentity(
        "kimi-code",
        "kimi",
        "kimi-code/k3",
      ),
      async *invoke(task) {
        yield structuredReviewDoneEvent(task);
      },
    }),
  ];
}

function fakeAdapter(options: {
  id: string;
  kinds: CapabilityKind[];
  identity: AgentRuntimeIdentity;
  invoke: AgentAdapter["invoke"];
}): AgentAdapter {
  const capabilities: Capability[] = options.kinds.map((kind) =>
    CapabilitySchema.parse({
      id: `${options.id}.${kind}`,
      kind,
      agent_id: options.id,
      description: `${options.id} ${kind}`,
      cost_tier: "mid",
    }),
  );
  return {
    id: options.id,
    display_name: options.id,
    cost_tier: "mid",
    capabilities: () => capabilities,
    isAvailable: async () => ({
      available: true,
      last_checked_at: 1,
    }),
    runtimeIdentity: async () => options.identity,
    invoke: options.invoke,
  };
}

function runtimeIdentity(
  adapterId: string,
  providerId: string,
  modelId: string,
): AgentRuntimeIdentity {
  return {
    adapter_id: adapterId,
    adapter_version: "1.0.0",
    auth_mode: "test",
    provider_id: providerId,
    model_id: modelId,
    evidence_source: "reported",
  };
}

function doneEvent(
  task: AgentTask,
  status: "completed" | "failed",
  summary: string,
): HermesEvent {
  return {
    event_id: `${task.run_id}:1`,
    seq: 1,
    run_id: task.run_id,
    session_id: task.session_id,
    created_at: Date.now() / 1000,
    terminal: true,
    type: "done",
    payload: {
      status,
      last_seq: 0,
      result_summary: summary,
    },
  };
}

function structuredReviewDoneEvent(task: AgentTask): HermesEvent {
  return {
    ...doneEvent(task, "completed", "approved"),
    payload: {
      status: "completed",
      last_seq: 0,
      result_summary: "approved",
      review: {
        schema_version: 1,
        verdict: "approved",
        risk: "read_only",
        markdown: "# Review\n\nApproved.",
        judgments: [],
        findings: [],
        recommended_actions: [],
        evidence_refs: [],
        runtime_identity: runtimeIdentity(
          "kimi-code",
          "kimi",
          "kimi-code/k3",
        ),
      },
    },
  };
}

describe("V2 real guarded-local host boundary", () => {
  it.each(["valid", "outside", "review_mutates", "governance", "policy_drift", "hidden_index", "new_ignored", "late_mutation"])("retains isolated candidate and fences verification: %s", async (mode) => {
    const outside = mode === "outside";
    const succeeds = mode === "valid";
    const workspace = await initGuardedFixture();
    const base = execFileSync("git", ["rev-parse", "HEAD"], { cwd: workspace, encoding: "utf8" }).trim();
    const original = await readFile(join(workspace, "src/greeting.ts"), "utf8");
    const contractPath = join(tmp, "contract.json");
    await writeFile(contractPath, JSON.stringify({ schema_version: 2, contract_id: "contract-test", task_id: "task-contract-test",
      canonical_repo: realpathSync(workspace), mode: "implement", base_head: base, goal: "Add guarded fixture marker",
      required_checks: [{ id: "tests", registry_ref: deliveryCheckReference("pnpm test") }], review_policy_ref: "profile:mac-mini",
      requested_completion: "local_verified", allowed_paths: ["src"], requested_actions: ["build", "verify"], budget_ref: `policy:${"b".repeat(64)}`,
      policy_digest: "b".repeat(64), publication_scope: { mode: "none" }, merge_authority: { mode: "none" } }));
    let candidatePath: string | undefined;
    const builder = async function* (task: AgentTask): AsyncIterable<HermesEvent> {
      const repo = String(task.context?.["repo_path"]); candidatePath = repo;
      await writeFile(join(repo, "src/greeting.ts"), "\nexport const guardedV2 = true;\n", { flag: "a" });
      if (outside) await writeFile(join(repo, "forbidden.txt"), "unexpected");
      if (mode === "hidden_index") execFileSync("git", ["update-index", "--assume-unchanged", "src/greeting.ts"], { cwd: repo });
      if (mode === "new_ignored") {
        const exclude = execFileSync("git", ["rev-parse", "--git-path", "info/exclude"], { cwd: repo, encoding: "utf8" }).trim();
        await writeFile(resolve(repo, exclude), "\nsrc/hidden.ts\n", { flag: "a" });
        await writeFile(join(repo, "src/hidden.ts"), "hidden candidate");
      }
      if (mode === "governance") await writeFile(join(repo, "src/secret.ts"), 'const api_key = "sk-test-secret-token";\n');
      await writeFile(contractPath, "{}"); // Later source edits cannot replace the frozen admission contract.
      yield doneEvent(task, "completed", "builder completed");
    };
    const adapters = guardedAdapters(builder);
    const prompts = new Map<string, string>();
    const contextReceipts: import("./execution-context.js").ExecutionContextReceipt[] = [];
    if (mode === "review_mutates") adapters[1]!.invoke = async function* (task) {
      await writeFile(join(candidatePath!, "src/greeting.ts"), "\nexport const afterCheck = true;\n", { flag: "a" });
      yield structuredReviewDoneEvent(task);
    };
    for (const adapter of adapters) {
      const invoke = adapter.invoke;
      adapter.invoke = async function* (task, signal) { prompts.set(task.run_id, task.prompt); yield* invoke.call(adapter, task, signal); };
    }
    let policyReads = 0;
    try {
      const code = await handleDeliverCommand(["--workspace", workspace, "--profile", "mac-mini", "--autonomy", "guarded-local", "--contract", contractPath, ...(succeeds ? ["--execution-context", join(tmp, "context.json")] : []), "--json"], {
        ...createDeliverCommandDeps(), adapters: () => adapters,
        createLoopGovernanceEvidence: async (...input) => {
          if (mode === "late_mutation" && input[1] === candidatePath)
            await writeFile(join(candidatePath!, "src/greeting.ts"), "\nexport const lateMutation = true;\n", { flag: "a" });
          return createDeliverCommandDeps().createLoopGovernanceEvidence(...input);
        },
        createExecutionContext: async input => {
          const { createExecutionContextAdapters } = await import("./execution-context.js");
          return createExecutionContextAdapters({ ...input, mode: "local", loadContext: async () => [], recordReceipt: async receipt => {
            contextReceipts.push(receipt); return persistDeliveryHostRecord(join(tmp, "context-records"), receipt);
          } });
        },
        deliveryContractPolicy: async () => ({ policyDigest: (mode === "policy_drift" && ++policyReads > 1 ? "c" : "b").repeat(64), verifyCommands: ["pnpm test"], budgetRef: `policy:${"b".repeat(64)}`, budget: { max_builder_iterations: 1, max_review_rounds: 1, max_wall_time_ms: 60000, max_no_progress_rounds: 1 } }),
      });
      expect(code, `logs=${logs.join("\n")} errors=${errors.join("\n")}`).toBe(succeeds ? 0 : 2);
      expect(execFileSync("git", ["rev-parse", "HEAD"], { cwd: workspace, encoding: "utf8" }).trim()).toBe(base);
      expect(await readFile(join(workspace, "src/greeting.ts"), "utf8")).toBe(original);
      expect(candidatePath).not.toBe(workspace);
      expect(await readFile(join(candidatePath!, "src/greeting.ts"), "utf8")).toContain("guardedV2");
      const summary = JSON.parse(logs.join("\n"));
      expect(summary.delivery_contract.achieved_completion).toBe(succeeds ? "local_verified" : null);
      if (succeeds) {
        const receipt = JSON.parse(await readFile(summary.delivery_contract.verification_receipt.path, "utf8"));
        expect(receipt.source).toBe("osstack_loop_host");
        expect(contextReceipts.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(4);
        expect(receipt.context_receipt_refs).toHaveLength(contextReceipts.length);
        for (const recorded of contextReceipts) {
          const prompt = prompts.get(recorded.run_id)!;
          expect(recorded.prompt_sha256).toBe(deliveryBytesDigest(prompt));
          expect(recorded.contract_digest).toBe(summary.delivery_contract.digest);
          expect(recorded.repo_path).toBe(realpathSync(workspace));
          expect(recorded.evidence_boundary).toBe("agent_task_prompt_not_provider_wire");
          if (recorded.adapter_id === "claude-code") expect(prompt.split(summary.delivery_contract.brief)).toHaveLength(2);
        }
        expect(receipt.checks).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining([expect.objectContaining({ id: "tests", exit_code: 0 })]));
        expect(receipt.candidate.tree_id).not.toBe(execFileSync("git", ["rev-parse", "HEAD^{tree}"], { cwd: workspace, encoding: "utf8" }).trim());
        expect(execFileSync("git", ["rev-parse", "HEAD"], { cwd: candidatePath, encoding: "utf8" }).trim()).toBe(base);
      }
    } finally {
      if (candidatePath) execFileSync("git", ["worktree", "remove", "--force", candidatePath], { cwd: workspace });
    }
  }, 60000);
});


FILE packages/cli/src/guarded-git-runtime.test.ts
import { execFileSync } from "node:child_process";

  mkdir,
  readFile,
  rm,
  writeFile,
} from "node:fs/promises";

const SHA_A = "a".repeat(64);
const SHA_B = "b".repeat(64);
let root: string;
let previousHome: string | undefined;

beforeEach(() => {
  root = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "osstack-guarded-git-"));
  previousHome = process.env["OSSTACK_HOME"];
  process.env["OSSTACK_HOME"] = join(root, "home");
});

afterEach(async () => {
  if (previousHome === undefined) {
    delete process.env["OSSTACK_HOME"];
  } else {
    process.env["OSSTACK_HOME"] = previousHome;
  }
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
  await rm(root, { recursive: true, force: true });

describe("GuardedGitRuntime", () => {
  it.each([
    ["R1 documentation", "docs/readme.md"],
    ["R2 source", "src/app.ts"],
  ])("completes %s through exact commit and final proof", async (
    _label,
    changedPath,
  ) => {
    const workspace = await fixtureRepo();
    const evidenceRoot = join(root, "evidence");
    const builderInvoke = vi.fn(async function* (
      task: AgentTask,
    ): AsyncIterable<HermesEvent> {
      const repoPath = String(task.context?.["repo_path"]);
      await mkdir(join(repoPath, changedPath.split("/")[0]!), {
        recursive: true,
      });
      await writeFile(
        join(repoPath, changedPath),
        "export const guarded = true;\n",
      );
      yield doneEvent(task, {
        status: "completed",
        last_seq: 0,
        result_summary: "built",
      });
    });
    const deliverGit = vi.fn(async () => ({
      pushedCommit: git(workspace, ["rev-parse", "HEAD"]),
      pr: {
        number: 12,
        url: "https://github.com/owner/repo/pull/12",
        reused: false,
      },
      mergeCommit: "c".repeat(40),
      mergedAt: "2026-07-28T19:00:00.000Z",
    }));
    const proofs: GuardedGitProofInput[] = [];
    const { runtime, close } = await testRuntime({
      workspace,
      evidenceRoot,
      builderInvoke,
      deliverGit,
      proofs,
    });

    try {
      const result = await runtime.start(startInput({
        workspace,
        evidenceRoot,
        expectedPaths: [changedPath],
      }));
      const loopState = await readFile(
        join(evidenceRoot, "LOOP-STATE.json"),
        "utf8",
      );

      expect(result.status, `${JSON.stringify(result, null, 2)}\n${loopState}`).toBe(
        "completed",
      );
      expect(result.run.state).toBe("completed");
      expect(builderInvoke).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
      expect(deliverGit).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
      expect(proofs.map((proof) => proof.stage)).toEqual([
        "candidate",
        "final",
      ]);
      expect(git(workspace, ["rev-list", "--count", "HEAD"])).toBe("2");
      expect(git(workspace, ["status", "--porcelain"])).toBe("");
      expect(
        git(workspace, ["show", "-s", "--format=%B", "HEAD"]),
      ).toContain("OSSTACK-IDEMPOTENCY-KEY:");
      await expect(
        readFile(join(evidenceRoot, "LOOP-STATE.json"), "utf8"),
      ).resolves.toContain("\"outcome\": \"success\"");
    } finally {
      close();
    }
  });

  it("pauses preflight R3 before invoking Builder", async () => {
    const workspace = await fixtureRepo();
    const evidenceRoot = join(root, "r3-preflight");
    const builderInvoke = vi.fn(async function* (
      task: AgentTask,
    ): AsyncIterable<HermesEvent> {
      yield doneEvent(task, {
        status: "completed",
        last_seq: 0,
        result_summary: "unexpected",
      });
    });
    const proofs: GuardedGitProofInput[] = [];
    const { runtime, close } = await testRuntime({
      workspace,
      evidenceRoot,
      builderInvoke,
      deliverGit: vi.fn(),
      proofs,
    });

    try {
      const result = await runtime.start(startInput({
        workspace,
        evidenceRoot,
        expectedPaths: ["src/auth/session.ts"],
      }));

      expect(result.status, JSON.stringify(result, null, 2)).toBe(
        "needs_approval",
      );
      expect(result.approval_request?.phase).toBe("preflight");
      expect(builderInvoke).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(proofs).toEqual([]);
      expect(git(workspace, ["rev-list", "--count", "HEAD"])).toBe("1");
    } finally {
      close();
    }
  });

  it("pauses a protected policy diff before proof, commit, or Git", async () => {
    const workspace = await fixtureRepo();
    const evidenceRoot = join(root, "r3-post-diff");
    const builderInvoke = vi.fn(async function* (
      task: AgentTask,
    ): AsyncIterable<HermesEvent> {
      const repoPath = String(task.context?.["repo_path"]);
      await mkdir(join(repoPath, ".osstack"), { recursive: true });
      await writeFile(
        join(repoPath, ".osstack/policy.yaml"),
        "mode: observe\nauto_apply: false\n",
      );
      yield doneEvent(task, {
        status: "completed",
        last_seq: 0,
        result_summary: "built",
      });
    });
    const deliverGit = vi.fn();
    const proofs: GuardedGitProofInput[] = [];
    const { runtime, close } = await testRuntime({
      workspace,
      evidenceRoot,
      builderInvoke,
      deliverGit,
      proofs,
    });

    try {
      const result = await runtime.start(startInput({
        workspace,
        evidenceRoot,
        expectedPaths: ["docs/readme.md"],
      }));
      const loopState = await readFile(
        join(evidenceRoot, "LOOP-STATE.json"),
        "utf8",
      );

      expect(result.status, `${JSON.stringify(result, null, 2)}\n${loopState}`).toBe(
        "needs_approval",
      );
      expect(result.approval_request?.phase).toBe("post_diff");
      expect(proofs).toEqual([]);
      expect(deliverGit).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(git(workspace, ["rev-list", "--count", "HEAD"])).toBe("1");
    } finally {
      close();
    }
  });

  it("resumes blocked Git delivery after restart without rebuilding or recommitting", async () => {
    const workspace = await fixtureRepo();
    const evidenceRoot = join(root, "restart");
    const builderInvoke = vi.fn(async function* (
      task: AgentTask,
    ): AsyncIterable<HermesEvent> {
      const repoPath = String(task.context?.["repo_path"]);
      await writeFile(join(repoPath, "README.md"), "# guarded\n");
      yield doneEvent(task, {
        status: "completed",
        last_seq: 0,
        result_summary: "built",
      });
    });
    let pullRequestExists = false;
    const firstDelivery = vi.fn(async () => {
      pullRequestExists = true;
      throw new Error("daemon stopped after PR creation");
    });
    const firstProofs: GuardedGitProofInput[] = [];
    const first = await testRuntime({
      workspace,
      evidenceRoot,
      builderInvoke,
      deliverGit: firstDelivery,
      proofs: firstProofs,
    });

    const blocked = await first.runtime.start(startInput({
      workspace,
      evidenceRoot,
      expectedPaths: ["README.md"],
    }));
    expect(blocked.status).toBe("blocked");
    expect(blocked.run.state).toBe("blocked_delivery");
    expect(pullRequestExists).toBe(true);
    expect(git(workspace, ["rev-list", "--count", "HEAD"])).toBe("2");
    first.close();

    const resumedBuilder = vi.fn(builderInvoke);
    const resumedDelivery = vi.fn(async () => {
      expect(pullRequestExists).toBe(true);
      return {
        pushedCommit: git(workspace, ["rev-parse", "HEAD"]),
        pr: {
          number: 12,
          url: "https://github.com/owner/repo/pull/12",
          reused: true,
        },
        mergeCommit: "c".repeat(40),
        mergedAt: "2026-07-28T19:00:00.000Z",
      };
    });
    const resumedProofs: GuardedGitProofInput[] = [];
    const second = await testRuntime({
      workspace,
      evidenceRoot,
      builderInvoke: resumedBuilder,
      deliverGit: resumedDelivery,
      proofs: resumedProofs,
      assertPolicyUnchanged: async () => {
        throw new Error("workspace policy is no longer the base policy");
      },
    });
    try {
      const result = await second.runtime.resume("run-1", undefined, {
        recoveryKind: "restart",
      });

      expect(result.status).toBe("completed");
      expect(result.run.state).toBe("completed");
      expect(resumedBuilder).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(resumedDelivery).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
      expect(git(workspace, ["rev-list", "--count", "HEAD"])).toBe("2");
      expect(resumedProofs.map((proof) => proof.stage)).toEqual([
        "final",
      ]);
      expect(
        listAutonomyTransitions(second.db, "run-1").filter(
          (transition) => (
            JSON.parse(transition.event_json) as {
              recovery?: { kind?: string };
            }
          ).recovery?.kind === "restart",
        ),
      ).toHaveLength(1);
    } finally {
      second.close();
    }
  });
});

async function fixtureRepo(): Promise<string> {
  const workspace = join(root, "repo");
  const remote = join(root, "remote.git");
  execFileSync("git", ["init", "--bare", remote]);
  await mkdir(join(workspace, "docs"), { recursive: true });
  await writeFile(join(workspace, "README.md"), "# fixture\n");
  execFileSync("git", ["init", "-b", "main"], { cwd: workspace });
  git(workspace, ["add", "."]);
  execFileSync(
    "git",
    [
      "-c",
      "user.name=osstack test",
      "-c",
      "user.email=osstack-test@example.invalid",
      "commit",
      "-m",
      "test: seed",
    ],
    { cwd: workspace },
  );
  git(workspace, ["remote", "add", "origin", remote]);
  git(workspace, ["push", "origin", "main"]);
  git(workspace, ["checkout", "-b", "codex/guarded-test"]);
  return workspace;
}

function git(workspace: string, args: string[]): string {
  return execFileSync("git", args, {
    cwd: workspace,
    encoding: "utf8",
  }).trim();
}

function startInput(options: {
  workspace: string;
  evidenceRoot: string;
  expectedPaths: string[];
}) {
  const routing = GuardedGitRouting.parse({
    schema_version: 1,
    task: {
      task_id: "task-1",
      capability: "code.write",
      prompt: "Implement guarded fixture",
      priority: 50,
      source: "manual",
      context: {},
    },
    profile: {
      profile_name: "test",
      reason: "test routing",
      builder_agent_id: "builder",
      evaluator_agent_id: "kimi-code",
      builder_identity: identity("builder", "anthropic", "claude"),
      evaluator_identity: identity("kimi-code", "kimi", "kimi-k3"),
      independent: true,
      independence_reason: "different provider",
    },
    verify_commands: ["node -e \"process.exit(0)\""],
    command_timeout_ms: 10_000,
    evidence_root: options.evidenceRoot,
    base_commit_sha: git(options.workspace, ["rev-parse", "HEAD"]),
    policy_sha256: SHA_A,
    max_auto_risk: "R2",
    protected_policy_paths: [
      ".osstack/repo.yaml",
      ".osstack/policy.yaml",
    ],
    approval_ttl_ms: 60_000,
    git: {
      remote: "origin",
      base: "main",
      branch: "codex/guarded-test",
      title: "feat: guarded test",
      body_file: join(options.evidenceRoot, "pull-request.md"),
      ci_timeout_ms: 10_000,
    },
  });
  return {
    autonomy_run_id: "run-1",
    task_id: "task-1",
    repo_id: "owner/repo",
    workspace_path: options.workspace,
    goal: "Implement guarded fixture",
    expected_paths: options.expectedPaths,
    requested_actions: ["build", "commit", "merge", "push", "verify"],
    contract_sha256: SHA_B,
    routing,
    budget: {
      max_builder_iterations: 2,
      max_review_rounds: 2,
      max_wall_time_ms: 60_000,
      max_no_progress_rounds: 1,
    },
  };
}

async function testRuntime(options: {
  RuntimeType?: typeof GuardedGitRuntime;
  omitPolicyGuard?: boolean;
  workspace: string;
  evidenceRoot: string;
  builderInvoke: AgentAdapter["invoke"];
  deliverGit: NonNullable<
    GuardedGitRuntimeConstructorDeps["deliverGit"]
  >;
  proofs: GuardedGitProofInput[];
  candidateDeliveryContext?: GuardedGitRuntimeConstructorDeps["candidateDeliveryContext"];
  candidateDeliveryRunner?: GuardedGitRuntimeConstructorDeps["candidateDeliveryRunner"];
  assertPolicyUnchanged?: NonNullable<
    GuardedGitRuntimeConstructorDeps["assertPolicyUnchanged"]
  >;
}) {
  await mkdir(options.evidenceRoot, { recursive: true });
  const dbPath = join(root, "home", "state.db");
  await mkdir(join(root, "home"), { recursive: true });
  const db = guardedOpenDb({ path: dbPath });
  const runtime = new (options.RuntimeType ?? GuardedGitRuntime)({
    db,
    dbPath,
    trustedApprovalPublicKeys: new Map(),
    adapters: () => adapters(options.builderInvoke),
    writeProof: async (input) => {
      options.proofs.push(input);
      const directory = join(
        options.evidenceRoot,
        `proof-${input.stage}`,
      );
      await mkdir(directory, { recursive: true });
      const manifestPath = join(directory, "manifest.json");
      await writeFile(
        manifestPath,
        `${JSON.stringify({ stage: input.stage })}\n`,
      );
      return {
        manifest_path: manifestPath,
        manifest_sha256:
          input.stage === "candidate" ? SHA_A : SHA_B,
      };
    },
    deliverGit: options.deliverGit,
    ...(options.candidateDeliveryContext === undefined ? {} : { candidateDeliveryContext: options.candidateDeliveryContext }),
    ...(options.candidateDeliveryRunner === undefined ? {} : { candidateDeliveryRunner: options.candidateDeliveryRunner }),
    ...(options.omitPolicyGuard ? {} : { assertPolicyUnchanged:
      options.assertPolicyUnchanged
      ?? (async () => undefined) }),
    evaluateGovernance: async () => ({
      blocking: false,
      evidence: { status: "pass" },
    }),
  });
  return { runtime, db, close: () => db.close() };
}

type GuardedGitRuntimeConstructorDeps =
  ConstructorParameters<typeof GuardedGitRuntime>[0];

function adapters(builderInvoke: AgentAdapter["invoke"]): AgentAdapter[] {
  return [
    adapter("builder", "code.write", identity(
      "builder",
      "anthropic",
      "claude",
    ), builderInvoke),
    adapter("kimi-code", "code.review", identity(
      "kimi-code",
      "kimi",
      "kimi-k3",
    ), async function* (task) {
      yield doneEvent(task, {
        status: "completed",
        last_seq: 0,
        result_summary: "approved",
        review: {
          schema_version: 1,
          verdict: "approved",
          risk: "read_only",
          markdown: "# Review\n\nApproved.",
          judgments: [],
          findings: [],
          recommended_actions: [],
          evidence_refs: [],
          runtime_identity: identity(
            "kimi-code",
            "kimi",
            "kimi-k3",
          ),
        },
      });
    }),
  ];
}

function adapter(
  id: string,
  kind: CapabilityKind,
  runtimeIdentity: AgentRuntimeIdentity,
  invoke: AgentAdapter["invoke"],
): AgentAdapter {
  return {
    id,
    display_name: id,
    cost_tier: "mid",
    capabilities: () => [CapabilitySchema.parse({
      id: `${id}.${kind}`,
      kind,
      agent_id: id,
      description: `${id} ${kind}`,
      cost_tier: "mid",
    })],
    isAvailable: async () => ({
      available: true,
      last_checked_at: 1,
    }),
    runtimeIdentity: async () => runtimeIdentity,
    invoke,
  };
}

function identity(
  adapterId: string,
  providerId: string,
  modelId: string,
): AgentRuntimeIdentity {
  return {
    adapter_id: adapterId,
    adapter_version: "1.0.0",
    auth_mode: "test",
    provider_id: providerId,
    model_id: modelId,
    evidence_source: "reported",
  };
}

function doneEvent(
  task: AgentTask,
  payload: unknown,
): HermesEvent {
  return HermesEventSchema.parse({
    event_id: `${task.run_id}:done`,
    seq: 1,
    run_id: task.run_id,
    session_id: task.session_id,
    created_at: Date.now() / 1_000,
    terminal: true,
    type: "done",
    payload,
  });
}

// Full existing guarded engine/local Git/proof flow. GH metadata and every delivery/merge are synthetic ports.
async function contextScenario(mode: string, RuntimeType?: typeof GuardedGitRuntime) {
  const workspace = await fixtureRepo(); const evidenceRoot = join(root, "context-evidence");
  const input = startInput({ workspace, evidenceRoot, expectedPaths: ["docs/readme.md"] });
  const original = structuredClone(input);
  const context = { environmentFingerprint: "c".repeat(64), policyFingerprint: "d".repeat(64), testsFingerprint: "e".repeat(64) };
  const calls: Array<{ command: string; args: readonly string[] }> = [];
  let sampleCount = 0;
  const sampledInputs: Array<{ routing: GuardedGitRouting; budget: typeof input.budget }> = [];
  const sample = vi.fn(async (value: { routing: GuardedGitRouting; budget: typeof input.budget }) => {
    sampleCount++; sampledInputs.push(structuredClone(value));
    if (mode === "undefined" || mode === "undefined-policy-drift" || (["withdrawn", "withdrawn-policy-drift"].includes(mode) && sampleCount > 1)) return undefined;
    if (mode === "throw") throw new Error("synthetic callback private marker must not echo");
    if (mode === "invalid") return { ...context, unexpected: true };
    if (mode === "raw-invalid") return null as unknown as typeof context;
    value.routing.git.branch = "mutated-not-authoritative"; value.budget.max_review_rounds = 999;
    return mode === "changed" && sampleCount > 1 ? { ...context, testsFingerprint: "f".repeat(64) } : context;
  });
  const runner: NonNullable<GuardedGitRuntimeConstructorDeps["candidateDeliveryRunner"]> = { run: async (command, args) => {
    calls.push({ command, args: [...args] });
    const head = git(workspace, ["rev-parse", "HEAD"]);
    const ok = (stdout: string) => ({ stdout, stderr: "", exitCode: 0 });
    if (command === "git") {
      if (args[0] === "remote" && args[1] === "get-url") return ok("https://github.com/owner/repo.git");
      if (args[0] === "ls-remote" && ["stale-head", "stale-policy-drift"].includes(mode)) return ok(`${original.routing.base_commit_sha}\trefs/heads/codex/guarded-test\n${original.routing.base_commit_sha}\trefs/heads/main\n`);
      if (args[0] === "ls-remote" && mode === "badrefs") return ok(`${head}\trefs/heads/foreign\n`);
      if (args[0] === "ls-remote") return ok(`${head}\trefs/heads/codex/guarded-test\n${original.routing.base_commit_sha}\trefs/heads/main\n`);
      if (["check-ref-format", "branch", "status", "rev-parse"].includes(args[0] ?? "")) return ok(git(workspace, [...args]));
      throw new Error("observer attempted non-read Git command");
    }
    if (command === "gh" && args[0] === "repo" && args[1] === "view") return ok(JSON.stringify({ nameWithOwner: "owner/repo" }));
    if (command === "gh" && args[0] === "api") {
      const endpoint = args.find(v => v.startsWith("repos/")) ?? "";
      if (endpoint.includes("/rules/branches/")) return ok("[]");
      if (endpoint.includes("/branches/codex%2Fguarded-test/protection")) return { stdout: "", stderr: "HTTP 404: Branch not protected", exitCode: 1 };
      if (endpoint.includes("/branches/main/protection")) return ok(JSON.stringify({ required_status_checks: { contexts: ["ci"], checks: [] }, required_pull_request_reviews: {} }));
    }
    if (command === "gh" && args[0] === "pr" && args[1] === "view") {
      if (args.includes("headRefOid,baseRefName,headRefName")) return ok(JSON.stringify({ headRefOid: head, baseRefName: "main", headRefName: "codex/guarded-test" }));
      const metadata = { number: 12, url: "https://github.com/owner/repo/pull/12", headRefOid: head, state: "OPEN" };
      if (args.includes("number,url,headRefOid,state")) return ok(JSON.stringify(metadata));
      if (args.includes("number,url,headRefOid,state,mergeable,mergeStateStatus,statusCheckRollup")) return ok(JSON.stringify({ ...metadata, mergeable: "MERGEABLE", mergeStateStatus: "CLEAN", statusCheckRollup: [{ name: "ci", status: mode === "pending" ? "IN_PROGRESS" : "COMPLETED", conclusion: "SUCCESS" }] }));
    }
    throw new Error("synthetic runner denies unsupported or write command");
  } };
  const deliverGit = vi.fn(async (_input: Parameters<NonNullable<GuardedGitRuntimeConstructorDeps["deliverGit"]>>[0]) => ({ pushedCommit: git(workspace, ["rev-parse", "HEAD"]), pr: { number: 12, url: "https://github.com/owner/repo/pull/12", reused: true }, mergeCommit: "c".repeat(40), mergedAt: "2026-07-28T19:00:00.000Z" }));
  const builderInvoke = vi.fn(async function* (task: AgentTask): AsyncIterable<HermesEvent> {
    const repo = String(task.context?.["repo_path"]); await mkdir(join(repo, "docs"), { recursive: true }); await writeFile(join(repo, "docs/readme.md"), "synthetic guarded edit");
    yield doneEvent(task, { status: "completed", last_seq: 0, result_summary: "built" });
  });
  const guard = vi.fn(async () => {
    if ((mode === "undefined-policy-drift" && sampleCount === 1) || (mode === "withdrawn-policy-drift" && sampleCount === 2) || (mode === "stale-policy-drift" && sampleCount === 2)) throw new Error("SYNTHETIC_POLICY_CHANGED_AFTER_SAMPLE");
  });
  const { runtime, close } = await testRuntime({ ...(mode === "missing-policy-guard" ? { omitPolicyGuard: true } : {}), ...(RuntimeType === undefined ? {} : { RuntimeType }), workspace, evidenceRoot, builderInvoke, deliverGit, proofs: [], assertPolicyUnchanged: guard, ...(mode === "absent" ? {} : { candidateDeliveryContext: sample }), candidateDeliveryRunner: runner });
  try { const result = await runtime.start(input); return { result, calls, sample, deliverGit, guard, input, original, context, sampledInputs }; } finally { close(); }
}
